- **find-case-by-party-verify**: Find a UK court judgment by party names and verify its neutral citation before citing it to the user. Use whenever the user asks "find &lt;X v Y&gt;", "get me the citation for &lt;case&gt;", "what's the neutral citation for the &lt;case name&gt;", "is there a case called &lt;name&gt;", or pastes a partial case reference. This skill verifies the case exists in TNA Find Case Law BEFORE reporting the citation — prevents the "plausible-looking but wrong" citation failure that happens when an agent formats from training data. ([uk-agents/uk-legal-plugins](https://github.com/uk-agents/uk-legal-plugins/tree/HEAD/law-student-uk/skills/find-case-by-party-verify) · ★19 · 2 copies)

- **find-causal-floor**: Diagnose a reported symptom without changing the system, by tracing evidence-backed necessary conditions down to the lowest actionable in-scope causal floor or a named external authority boundary. Use for recurring or cross-cutting defects, uncertain ownership, conflicting implementations, incident diagnosis, failure-chain analysis, or any explicit root-cause request — before any design work. Produces the verified floor and sourced invariants that design-from-invariants consumes. ([seattled23/cheese](https://github.com/seattled23/cheese/tree/HEAD/skills/find-causal-floor))
- **find-cause**: Use when a failure or friction keeps coming back after a fix - a repeat incident, a recurring CI or build break, the same review comment raised again, a Retrospective event firing a second time, or when someone asks why this keeps happening. Not for debugging a single live bug in progress. Personal, non-technical judgment calls route to `guide-decision`/`guide-purchase`. ([NormandErwan/claude-casebook](https://github.com/NormandErwan/claude-casebook/tree/HEAD/plugins/find-cause/skills/find-cause) · ★1)
- **find-cause**: The craft of finding a cause: reproduce, isolate, read, state the cause with evidence, fix the cause, run again. Use when a check fails, a test is red, a result is unexpected or a bug is reported, and the cause is not clear after one read. ([lamtran8401/nook](https://github.com/lamtran8401/nook/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/find-cause))
- **find-cause**: Cause before any fix (เทสต์ไม่ผ่าน / บั๊ก / พัง / error / gate FAIL / CI แดง / หน้าเพี้ยน): reproduce with the smallest command, narrow down, state the cause with evidence, then fix and re-run the same command. Use whenever a test, setup, gate, CI, ui_smoke or page fails, or the owner reports a bug. ([nuttratts-code/automon-arena](https://github.com/nuttratts-code/automon-arena/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/find-cause))
